# Commands · R · part 5

1,433 entries, repo-research-analyst to research-review. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **repo-research-analyst**: 1. **Investigate** — Read code, plans, PRs, git history, tests, docs to answer the question 2. **Analyze** — Identify patterns, gaps, contradictions, drift between plans and shipped code 3. **Report** — Write persistent report to `.context/reports/` or `.context/reviews/` 4. **Summarize** — Return findings to the caller (concise, actionable) ([jtouley/md_data_explorer](https://github.com/jtouley/md_data_explorer/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/repo-research-analyst.md))
- **repo-review**: Systematic whole-repo bug sweep across 8 dimensions (correctness, security, concurrency, resource-leaks, error-handling, performance, ci-cd-actions-security, dead-code-simplification), risk-sampled and effort-tiered, findings categorized P0-P3, with optional cross-model verification, a confirmed-only auto-fix pass, and an opt-in --converge loop that re-sweeps until no P0-P3 findings remain (or no further auto-fixable progress is possible). Not a diff/PR reviewer — that's the built-in /code-review. ([mcorbett51090/RavenClaude](https://github.com/mcorbett51090/RavenClaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/ravenclaude-core/commands/repo-review.md) · ★7)
- **repo-review**: Whole-repo code-quality audit — walks the codebase, dispatches repo-quality-reviewer agents in parallel, aggregates findings. Looks for duplicated logic, dead code, convention drift, oversized files, tight coupling, ignored warnings. Read-only. ([FroncekB/pell_skills](https://github.com/FroncekB/pell_skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/pell/commands/repo-review.md) · ★1)
- **repo-review**: Review a repository or subsystem's current state and return prioritized quality, maintainability, and operational findings. ([alvarovillalbaa/plugins](https://github.com/alvarovillalbaa/plugins/blob/HEAD/engineering/commands/repo-review.md))
- **repo-scan**: Produce a structured snapshot of the target repository for rapid task analysis. ([agentic-swe/agentic-swe](https://github.com/agentic-swe/agentic-swe/blob/HEAD/commands/repo-scan.md) · ★5)
- **repo-scan**: Scan the whole repository's architecture with Antigravity (agy) and report rule violations, layering breaches, and cyclic dependencies ([chanwoo040531/antigravity-plugin-cc](https://github.com/chanwoo040531/antigravity-plugin-cc/blob/HEAD/plugins/agy/commands/repo-scan.md))
- **repo-scan**: Cross-stack source code asset audit — classifies every file, detects embedded third-party libraries, and delivers actionable four-level verdicts per module with interactive HTML reports. ([ziyabicilecommerce-hub/ai-cash-machine](https://github.com/ziyabicilecommerce-hub/ai-cash-machine/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/repo-scan.md))
- **repo-screen**: Schneller Relevanz-Check für GitHub Repos bevor Deep Analysis. ([PrimeLineDirekt/Evolving-Template](https://github.com/PrimeLineDirekt/Evolving-Template/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/repo-screen.md))
- **repo-security-review**: Whole-repo security audit — walks the codebase, dispatches repo-security-reviewer agents in parallel, aggregates findings. Two passes per file: regex scan for sensitive data (SSNs, credit cards, API keys, JWTs, private keys), then code-level vulnerability review (XSS, SQLi, path traversal, crypto misuse, PII logging). Read-only. Findings include the literal matched value so you can verify each hit is real. ([FroncekB/pell_skills](https://github.com/FroncekB/pell_skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/pell/commands/repo-security-review.md) · ★1)
